Let's design balancing iteration:
```
def opt_iter(a, S, beta, eta, rng):
    q=conv(a); qmax=q.max(); w=softmax(q,beta); h=hankel(a,w); mean=h.mean(); r=h/(mean+eps); cand=a * np.power(r, -eta) ; normalize; return cand, qmax,q
```

```
def softmax_weights(q, beta):
    if beta <= 0: return np.ones(len(q))/len(q)
    x = q - q.max()
    # if beta large, underflow for many; ok.
    e = np.exp(beta*x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: # fallback top at max
       w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else w=e/s
    return w
```
But for beta very large and many max ties, okay. We may use double; x ≤0. Use beta cap maybe 10000.

Multiplicative update function:
```
def multiply_update(a, h, eta, S):
    mean = h.mean()
    if mean < 1e-300: return normalize(a,S)
    r = h / mean
    log_r = np.log(np.clip(r, 1e-12, 1e12))
    log_r = np.clip(log_r, -30, 30)
    cand = a * np.exp(-eta*log_r)
    return normalize_sum(cand, S)
```
Need np.clip vector, no lambda.

Euclidean update:
```
def euclidean_update(a, h, step, S):
    g = h - h.mean()
    normg = np.linalg.norm(g)
    if normg<1e-30: return normalize(a,S)
    cand = a - (step* normg? )? We want step in coordinate units. h approx S/n ~0.015. A normalized direction maybe g/normg, step length e.g. 0.05*S/sqrt(n)?
    cand = a - step * g / max(normg,1e-300)
    return project_simplex(cand, S)
```
But scaling step with normalized gradient loses magnitude. Could use step = alpha*S/(normg+eps). This gives change O(S). Maybe too large. Use alpha 0.05-0.5. We'll try line search. Project simple by approximate? Need exact.

Simplex projection implementation optimized:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    if v.size==0: return v
    if np.all(v <= 0): return ones*S/n
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cw=np.cumsum(w)
    rho=np.arange(1,v.size+1)
    theta=(cw-S)/rho
    valid=theta <= w
    rho_val=np.where(valid)[0].max()+1 if any else 0
    theta=(cw[rho_val-1]-S)/rho_val
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Potential overflow if v large; but v within S. Use float64.

Could have exact projection slow; use for occasional Euclidean. For multiplicative no projection.

Normalize_sum:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.full_like(a,S/len(a))
    return a*(S/s)
```
No cap 1000 needed? If sum S ≤√2*32768? For n=32768, S=256, max entries could maybe >1000? Need check. p_i from updates normalized with sum S; if distribution concentrated, max could >1000? Could happen. Evaluate clamps to 1000 but if >1000, our internal q using unclamped differs. We should enforce cap 1000 to match evaluator? We can clip and renormalize? Perturb function:
```
def perturb(a, S, rng, strength):
    noise = rng.normal(0, strength/np.sqrt(n), n)
    cand = normalize(a+noise,S)
    # maybe also mix with uniform/asymmetric
    return cand
```

- `convolve_full(a)` returns real full conv.

- `evaluate_fast(a)` returns qmax (FFT) for sum S? It should normalize? We keep sum S. Could compute sum and scale for safety: qmax objective = 2n max(conv(a))/sum^2. Use this for arbitrary arrays. More robust:
```
def fast_objective(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01: inf; c=conv_full(a); return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s))
```
This matches evaluator (except c max maybe negative? max includes positives). Use for all evaluations. For internal optimization fixed sum S, objective = c.max() (if sum S). But due numerical, use fast_objective.
- In optimize, we can normalize to sum S after each update; objective=conv cmax. But to be robust, compute obj = 2n*max/sum^2 (same). That can handle sum drift.

Selecting final best by fast objective, not evaluate, but final call evaluate.

FFT size caching:
```
def fft_size_for(n):
    L=2*n-1
    return 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
```

Pseudo full optimize:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, max_time, rng, label=""):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    n=a0.size
    if n<4: fallback
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_to_sum(a0,S)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_obj=fast_objective(a)  # ~qmax
    cur_obj=best_obj
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max_time
    beta=5.0
    eta=0.25
    temp=0.002
    no_improve=0
    total_improve=0
    last_print=start
    # precompute beta sequence? maybe
    while time.monotonic() < max(deadline-2, start):
        it +=1
        # ensure current objective maybe due normalize
        # compute q and qmax
        c=conv_full(a); q=c  # full
        cur_obj = 2*n*max(q)/sum(a)**2? since normalized sum S, use qmax.
        # update best
        if cur_obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj=cur_obj; best_a=a.copy()
            no_improve=0; temp=min(0.01,temp*1.5)? or keep
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        # choose weights
        # every 200 iters broad beta=1?
        use_broad = (it % 200 < 10)
        if use_broad:
            beta_use = 1.0
        else:
             # beta increases as no_improve? 
             beta_use = min(20000.0, 3.0 + 0.2*it + 0.5*no_improve)
        w=softmax_weights(q,beta_use)
        h=hankel_product(a,w)
        # if h all equal or bad, perturb weights? maybe if std small
        hv = h.std()/(h.mean()+1e-300)
        if hv < 1e-7:
            # try low beta and random weight? 
            w=uniform weights? or top? If uniform, h constant. Need create nonconstant weights by emphasizing low q lags? Use beta negative? 
            # We can use weights proportional to (qmin + eps - q) to accent edges? But may counter objective.
            # For now add noise to h: h += rng.normal(...)
```
If h variation small, gradient direction zero. For p constant, any weight's h constant? For uniform p, h_i=∑_j p_j w_{i+j}. If w_{i+j} sum weighted by p constant=0.5, constant. To get direction, need w such that sum over valid i for each j not constant. But for i variable, h_i=∑_j w_{i+j}p_j; if p constant, h_i = c * ∑_j w_{i+j}. Now, resample_sequence for upsample:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    n=len(a)
    if n==1: return ...
    t_old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    t_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    return np.interp(t_new,t_old,a)
```

```
def random_positive_sequence(n, rng, S):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    rtype=rng.integers(0,5)
    if rtype==0:
       base=(t+1e-6)**(-0.5)
       base *= (1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
    elif rtype==1:
       base=np.exp(rng.uniform(-1,1)*t)
       base += 0.3*np.exp(-((t-rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))/rng.uniform(0.05,0.2))**2)
    elif rtype==2:
       f1=rng.uniform(0.5,4); f2=rng.uniform(1,8); base=1+0.4*np.sin(2πf1t+φ)+0.25*np.cos(2πf2t+φ2)
    elif rtype==3:
       base=(1-t+1e-6)**(-0.5)
       base *= (1+0.4*np.cos(2πf t+φ))
    else:
       base=rng.random(n)+0.05
    base=np.maximum(base,1e-6)
    return normalize_to_sum(base,S)
```

```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label=""):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).reshape(-1)
    n=len(a0)
    if n<16: return a0, qmax? 
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_to_sum(a0,S)
    best_a=a.copy()
    c=conv_full(a); best_obj=c.max(); cur_obj=best_obj
    start=time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= start: return best_a,best_obj
    beta=5.0; max_beta=20000.0
    eta=0.3
    temp=0.001
    stuck=0
    it=0
    last_print=start
    print... 
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       it+=1
       # compute current q
       q=conv_full(a); qmax=q.max()
       if not finite: perturb
       cur_obj=qmax
       if cur_obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
           best_obj=cur_obj; best_a=a.copy(); stuck=0; temp=min(0.005,temp*1.2)
       else:
           stuck +=1
       # choose beta
       if it % 256 < 16:
           beta_use = 1.0
       else:
           beta_use = beta
           if stuck > 100:
              beta_use = min(max_beta, beta_use * 2.0)
       w=softmax_weights(q,beta_use)
       h=hankel_product(a,w)
       # Build candidates and line search
       candidates=[]
       meanh=np.mean(h)
       if meanh > 1e-300:
           d=(h-meanh)/meanh
           d=np.clip(d,-5.0,5.0)
           # Multiplicative candidates: eta*0.25, eta, eta*4
           for e in (max(0.01,eta*0.25), eta, min(4.0,eta*4.0)):
                arg=-e*d
                arg=np.clip(arg,-18,18)
                cand=a*np.exp(arg)
                cand=normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
                candidates.append(cand)
           # Euclidean candidates occasionally
           if it % 8 == 0:
               g=h-meanh
               normg=np.linalg.norm(g)
               if normg > 1e-20:
                   scale = S / max(normg, S/float(n)) #?
                   for lam in (0.05, 0.3):
                       cand = a - (lam*scale)*g
                       cand = project_simplex(cand, S)
                       # add small floor
                       cand = cand + 1e-12*S/float(n)
                       cand = normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
                       candidates.append(cand)
       else:
           candidates=[]
       best_cand=None; best_cand_obj=cur_obj
       for cand in candidates:
           c2=conv_full(cand); o2=c2.max()
           if o2 < best_cand_obj - 1e-13:
               best_cand_obj=o2; best_cand=cand
           if o2 < best_obj - 1e-13:
               best_obj=o2; best_a=cand.copy()
       if best_cand is not None and (best_cand_obj < cur_obj - 1e-13 or best_cand_obj < cur_obj + temp):
           a=best_cand; cur_obj=best_cand_obj
           # maybe adjust eta based on all? We don't know which cand. Could keep.
           stuck=0
           if best_cand_obj < old_cur:
               beta=min(max_beta,beta*1.01)
               eta=min(2.0,eta*1.02)
           else:
               temp*=0.7
       else:
           # no accept
           if eta > 0.05:
               eta *= 0.6
           else:
               eta *= 1.5
           if stuck > 100:
               temp *= 0.8
       # stuck large perturb
       if stuck > 300 or (stuck>0 and it % 2000 == 5):
           # multiple perturb tries, pick best
           best_pert=a.copy(); best_pert_obj=cur_obj
           for _ in range(4):
              if rng.random()<0.5:
                 noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02, n)? scale relative
                 cand = normalize_to_sum(a + rng.normal(0, 0.02*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
              else:
                 rand = random_positive_sequence(n,rng,S)
                 mix = (1-0.3)*a + 0.3*rand
                 cand=normalize_to_sum(mix,S)
              c2=conv_full(cand); o2=c2.max()
              if o2 < best_pert_obj:
                 best_pert_obj=o2; best_pert=cand
           a=best_pert; cur_obj=best_pert_obj
           if cur_obj < best_obj: update best...
           beta=max(1.0,beta*0.5)
           eta=0.3
           temp=0.003
           stuck=100? maybe 0? Keep stuck not reset fully to avoid immediate perturb.
       # time print
       if time.monotonic()-last_print > 20:
           print(label, it, best_obj, cur_obj, beta, eta)
           last_print=...
    # final check
    c=conv_full(best_a); return best_a, float(c.max())
```

Now, `project_simplex` for Euclidean. Let's implement robust but maybe unused often.
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    # if sum already S and v>=0 maybe return normalized
    pos_sum=np.sum(np.maximum(v,0.0))
    if pos_sum <= 0:
        return np.full(n, S/float(n))
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cw=np.cumsum(w)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    theta=(cw-S)/idx
    valid=theta <= w
    # due floating, if none, rho=n
    where=np.where(valid)[0]
    if where.size==0:
        rho=n
    else:
        rho=int(where[-1]+1)
    theta=(cw[rho-1]-S)/rho
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    return out
```
This uses sort. Fine.

Maybe add after Euclidean floor: if many zeros, add small. But `normalize_to_sum` doesn't add. We can in candidate:
```
cand[np.isnan...]=0
cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
cand += 1e-12 * S / n
cand = normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
```
1e-12*S/n ~1e-11, negligible.

Now, fast_objective for arbitrary a. It uses `conv_full`, but if a large length and sum zero. Ensure finite.
```
def fast_objective(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    c=conv_full(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*len(a)*m/(s*s))
```
For internal normalized sequences sum ~128, no need 1000 clip but ok. Clipping could change if a>1000. We avoid.

But in optimizer, we call `conv_full(cand).max()` for objective assuming sum S. If we use `fast_objective(cand)` for candidates, it clips but uses sum; consistent. Slight overhead sum. Use cmax for speed but if some cand normalized to S exactly, objective=cmax. To be safe, compute `cmax = float(c.max()); obj=2*n*cmax/(s*s)`. But s=S+eps. Use s? We can maintain sum by normalize; use cmax. For final global validation, fast_objective.

Detailed code:
```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

def _fft_len_for(n):
    L=2*n-1
    return 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
```
If n=1, L=1, (0).bit_length=0 ->1<<0=1. OK.

`convolve_full`:
```
def convolve_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==1:
       return np.array([float(a[0]*a[0])])
    size=_fft_len_for(n)
    A=fft(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False), size)
    c=ifft(A*A)
    return c.real[:2*n-1]
```
If a complex? no.

`hankel_product`:
```
def hankel_product(a, w):
    n=a.size
    if n==1:
       return np.array([float(a[0]*w[0])])? For h0= p0w0.
    size=_fft_len_for(n)
    R=w[::-1]
    A=fft(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False), size)
    W=fft(R.astype(np.float64, copy=False), size)
    c=ifft(A*W)
    return c.real[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
If n=1, c[n-1:2] length1? size=1? Actually size=1, w length1, R length1, c real[0], n-1=0, 2n-1=1, slice [0:1], reverse ok. General works if size >= L. So no special.

`normalize_to_sum`:
```
def normalize_to_sum(a,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       return np.full(a.shape,S/float(a.size),dtype=float)
    return a*(S/s)
```
If a.size=0? Avoid. In functions ensure.

`project_simplex`: as above. If v size 0 return.

`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(q,beta):
    L=q.size
    if L==0: return np.array([])
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    m=q.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m):
       return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    e=np.exp(beta*(q-m))
    s=e.sum()
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0; return w
    return e/s
```

`power_sequence`:
```
def power_sequence(n,alpha,reverse,S):
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    if reverse:
       x=n+1-idx
    else:
       x=idx# Use 0.5 shift? idx is 1..n, so i+1. We may want i+0.5:
       # if not reverse: x=0.5+np.arange(n)
    # Let's use i+0.5:
    x=np.arange(0.5,n+0.5)
    if reverse: x=n+0.5 - np.arange(n)
    a=np.power(x,-alpha)
    return normalize_to_sum(a,S)
```
For alpha=0.5. If reverse with x=n+0.5 - arange: i=0 x=n+0.5 (low), i=n-1 x=0.5 (high). Good.

`random_positive_sequence` as above. Use normalize. If rng.integers not? `int(rng.integers(0,5))`.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n_new):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
    n=a.size
    if n_new <=0 or n<=1: return np.full(n_new, float(a[0]) if n else 0.0)
    if n_new == n: return a.copy()
    t_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    t_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    out=np.interp(t_new,t_old,a)
    return out
```

`update_global` top-level:
```
def update_global(a,best_obj,best_a):
    if a is None: return best_obj,best_a
    a=np.asarray(a,float).reshape(-1)
    if a.size==0: return best_obj,best_a
    obj=fast_objective(a)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
        return obj, normalize_to_sum(a, np.sqrt(2.0*a.size)) # store normalized?
    return best_obj,best_a
```
But if we store normalized, objective same. Use `normalize_to_sum`. However if a already normalized, ok.

`fast_objective` uses clip and sum; if a has negative, clip. Good.

Now, optimizer. Let's write robustly.

```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label=""):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float).reshape(-1)
    n=a0.size
    if n < 8:
       return a0, fast_objective(a0)
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_to_sum(a0,S)
    c0=convolve_full(a)
    best_obj=float(c0.max())
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj): best_obj=np.inf
    best_a=a.copy()
    cur=best_obj
    prev=best_obj
    if not np.isfinite(deadline): deadline=time.monotonic()+60
    start=time.monotonic()
    eta=0.3
    beta=5.0
    max_beta=30000.0
    temp=1e-4
    progress=0
    it=0
    last_print=start
    if label: print("optimize start %s n=%d obj=%.6f" % (label,n,best_obj))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it+=1
        q=convolve_full(a)
        cmax=float(q.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax):
            a=normalize_to_sum(a + rng.normal(0,1e-4, n),S)
            continue
        cur=cmax
        if cur < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj=cur; best_a=a.copy(); progress=0
        if cur < prev - 1e-12:
            progress=0
        else:
            progress += 1
        prev=cur
        # broad phase
        if it % 256 < 20:
            beta_use=1.0
        else:
            beta_use=beta
            if progress > 80:
                beta_use=min(max_beta, beta_use * (1.0 + 0.01*min(100, progress-80)))
        w=softmax_weights(q,beta_use)
        h=hankel_product(a,w)
        mean_h=float(np.mean(h))
        candidates=[]
        if mean_h > 1e-300:
            d=(h-mean_h)/mean_h
            d=np.clip(d,-5.0,5.0)
            multipliers = (max(0.01,eta*0.25), eta, min(4.0,eta*4.0))
            for em in multipliers:
                arg=-em*d
                arg=np.clip(arg,-18.0,18.0)
                cand=a*np.exp(arg)
                cand=normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
                candidates.append(cand)
            if it % 12 == 0:
                g=h-mean_h
                normg=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
                if normg > 1e-20:
                    scale = S / max(normg, S/float(n))
                    for lm in (0.05,0.3):
                        cand=a - (lm*scale)*g
                        cand=project_simplex(cand,S)
                        cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
                        cand += 1e-12*S/float(n)
                        cand=normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
                        candidates.append(cand)
        if candidates:
            best_cand=None
            best_cand_obj=cur
            # maybe sort? no
            for cand in candidates:
                c2=convolve_full(cand)
                o2=float(c2.max())
                if np.isfinite(o2):
                    if o2 < best_cand_obj - 1e-13:
                        best_cand_obj=o2; best_cand=cand
                    if o2 < best_obj - 1e-13:
                        best_obj=o2; best_a=cand.copy()
            if best_cand is not None and (best_cand_obj < cur - 1e-13 or best_cand_obj < cur + temp):
                a=best_cand
                cur=best_cand_obj
                if cur < prev - 1e-13:
                    eta=min(2.0, eta*1.05)
                    beta=min(max_beta, beta*1.02)
                    temp=min(5e-4, temp*1.1)
                else:
                    temp=max(1e-7, temp*0.5)
            else:
                if eta > 0.05:
                    eta *= 0.7
                else:
                    eta = min(2.0, eta*1.5)
                if progress > 50:
                    beta = min(max_beta, beta * 1.1)
        # perturb
        if progress > 350:
            best_pert=None
            best_pert_obj=cur
            for _ in range(5):
                r=rng.random()
                if r < 0.4:
                    scale = 0.02 * S / max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(n)))
                    cand = a + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
                elif r < 0.7:
                    rand = random_positive_sequence(n, rng, S)
                    cand = 0.8*a + 0.2*rand
                else:
                    floor = 1e-8*S/float(n)
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, n)
                    cand = a*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-5,5)) + floor
                cand=normalize_to_sum(cand,S)
                c2=convolve_full(cand)
                o2=float(c2.max())
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_pert_obj:
                    best_pert_obj=o2; best_pert=cand
            if best_pert is not None:
                a=best_pert
                cur=best_pert_obj
                if cur < best_obj:
                    best_obj=cur; best_a=a.copy()
            beta=max(1.0, beta*0.4)
            eta=0.3
            temp=3e-4
            progress=100  # not fully reset to allow more updates before next pert
        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            print("%s it=%d best=%.6f cur=%.6f progress=%d beta=%.1f eta=%.3f temp=%.2e" % (label,it,best_obj,cur,progress,beta,eta,temp))
            last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final
    c=convolve_full(best_a)
    bo=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(bo) or bo > best_obj:
       bo=best_obj
    return best_a, bo
```

Potential: `progress` starts 0; if no candidate accepted, increments. If broad phase every 256, okay.

`candidates` can include same? no.

```
if it%16==0: add one euclidean lam=0.2
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=1, budget_s=2400):
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    now=time.monotonic()
    global_deadline = now + max(60.0, budget_s - 45.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_obj=np.inf; best_a=None
    given=None
    try:
        g=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if g.size >= 32 and np.sum(np.clip(g,0,1000)) > 0:
            g=np.clip(g,0,1000)
            if g.size > 16384:
                g=resample_sequence(g,16384)
            given=g
    except Exception:
        given=None
    T=global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if T < 30:
       # fallback
       a=power_sequence(2048,0.5,False,np.sqrt(2*2048))
       return [float(x) for x in a]
    if given is not None:
       n1=given.size
       init1=given.copy()
       dur1=T*0.35 if best_obj inf? but no. Use 0.35; cap 700.
       dur1=max(60,min(dur1,700))
    else:
       n1=8192 if T>1200 else 4096
       init1=power_sequence(n1,0.5,False,np.sqrt(2*n1))
       dur1=max(120, min(T*0.45, 900))
    a1,s1=optimize_sequence(init1, time.monotonic()+dur1, rng, "phase1")
    best_obj,best_a=update_global(a1,best_obj,best_a)
    if best_a is not None: print("after phase1 n=%d obj=%.6f" % (len(best_a),best_obj))
    # phase2 larger
    rem=global_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180:
       if best_a is None:
          n2=8192
          init2=power_sequence(n2,0.5,False,np.sqrt(2*n2))
       else:
          n2=min(16384, max(2048, int(np.ceil(len(best_a)*1.5))) )
          # ensure maybe if len best <16384, double not less
          if n2 <= len(best_a): n2 = min(16384, len(best_a)+4096)
          if n2 > 16384: n2=16384
          # alternative: n2 = max(2*len best, 8192) capped 16384
          init2=resample_sequence(best_a,n2) if len(best_a)>1 else power_sequence(...)
       dur2=rem*0.45; dur2=max(120,min(dur2,1000))
       a2,s2=optimize_sequence(init2, time.monotonic()+dur2, rng, "phase2")
       update
    # phase3 if best length <16384 and rem > 180: n3=min(16384,2*len best or 16384)
    rem=...
    if rem > 180 and best_a is not None and len(best_a) < 16384:
        n3 = min(16384, max(2*len(best_a), len(best_a)+4096))
        init3=resample_sequence(best_a,n3)
        dur3 = min(rem*0.5, 600)
        optimize
    # final polish
    rem=global_deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 30 and best_a is not None:
       final_deadline = global_deadline - max(5, (global_deadline - time.monotonic())*0.02) # leave small
       a_f,s_f=optimize_sequence(best_a, final_deadline, rng, "final")
       update
    # If best None fallback power 2048
    if best_a is None:
       n=2048; best_a=power_sequence(n,0.5,False,np.sqrt(2*n))
    # maybe ensure not too large? if len>16384? cap? best from phases max 16384.
    best_a=np.clip(best_a,0,1000)
    s=best_a.sum(); if s<0.01 fallback
    # optional final evaluate
    try:
       real=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
       if np.isfinite(real):
          print("final evaluated %.6f" % real)
       else:
          print("eval inf")
    except Exception:
       pass
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```

`update_global`: As top-level:
```
def update_global(a, best_obj, best_a):
    if a is None:
       return best_obj,best_a
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size ==0: return best_obj,best_a
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*a.size)
    a=normalize_to_sum(a,S)
    obj=fast_objective(a)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
       return obj,a
    return best_obj,best_a
```
This normalizes. If a size huge? capped.

```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label=""):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a0.size < 8:
        return a0, fast_objective(a0)
    n=int(a0.size)
    S=float(np.sqrt(2.0*n))
    a=normalize_to_sum(a0,S)
    c0=convolve_full(a)
    best_obj=float(c0.max())
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj): best_obj=1e300
    best_a=a.copy()
    now=time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= now:
       return best_a,best_obj
    ...
```

```
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

    rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
```

```python
"""
Search nonnegative sequences minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Main ideas:
- Work on the normalized simplex sum(a)=sqrt(2n), so the objective is just
  max(a*a) computed by FFT self-convolution.
- Use a multiplicative natural-gradient / KKT-balancing update
  p_i <- p_i * exp(-eta * (H_i - mean(H))/mean(H)), where
  H_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j and w is a softmax over convolution lags.
- Line-search several eta values and occasionally use a Euclidean projected
  step. Improve on exact max-convolution objective with small temperature
  escapes and random restarts.
- Run multiple length scales, upsample the best candidate, and polish with a
  final stage.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def _fft_len_for(n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def convolve_full(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    size = _fft_len_for(n)
    A = fft(a, size)
    c = ifft(A * A)
    return c.real[:2 * n - 1]


def hankel_product(a, w):
    """
    Compute H_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j for i=0..n-1.
    This is the un-factor-2 gradient of weighted convolution sum.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = _fft_len_for(n)
    R = w[::-1]
    A = fft(a, size)
    W = fft(R, size)
    c = ifft(A * W)
    return c.real[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def normalize_to_sum(a, S):
    """Clip to nonnegative and rescale to exact sum S."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, S / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Exact projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=S}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cw = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (cw - S) / idx
    valid = theta <= w
    where = np.where(valid)[0]
    if where.size == 0:
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(where[-1] + 1)
    theta = (cw[rho - 1] - S) / float(rho)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def fast_objective(a):
    """Objective equivalent to evaluate_sequence, but via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * max(0.0, m) / (s * s))


def softmax_weights(q, beta):
    """Weights over convolution lags, emphasizing large lags."""
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    L = q.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp(beta * (q - m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def power_sequence(n, alpha, reverse, S):
    """A useful asymmetric one-sided power start."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(0.5, n + 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    if reverse:
        x = n + 0.5 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(x, -float(alpha))
    return normalize_to_sum(a, float(S))


def random_positive_sequence(n, rng, S):
    """Generate a random nonnegative smooth sequence ignored for diversity."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
    rtype = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    if rtype == 0:
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base = (t + 1e-6) ** (-0.5)
        base *= 1.0 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + ph)
    elif rtype == 1:
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 4.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 8.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base = 1.0
        base += 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + p1)
        base += 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t + p2)
    elif rtype == 2:
        u = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.85))
        sig = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.18))
        base = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.exp(-((t - u) / sig) ** 2)
    elif rtype == 3:
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        base = (1.0 - t + 1e-6) ** (-0.5)
        base *= 1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + ph)
    elif rtype == 4:
        k = max(3, int(n / 25))
        block = rng.uniform(0.25, 1.5, size=(k,))
        base = np.interp(t, np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, k, dtype=np.float64), block)
    else:
        base = rng.uniform(0.15, 1.0, size=n)
    base = np.where(np.isfinite(base), base, 0.1)
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-6)
    return normalize_to_sum(base, float(S))


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_new <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(t_new, t_old, a)


def update_global(a, best_obj, best_a):
    """Update best objective/array if a is better."""
    if a is None:
        return best_obj, best_a
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_obj, best_a
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * a.size))
    a = normalize_to_sum(a, S)
    obj = fast_objective(a)
    if np.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
        return obj, a
    return best_obj, best_a


def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label=""):
    """
    Local optimization of one sequence until deadline.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_objective) for sum=sqrt(2n) normalization.
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a0.size < 8:
        a0 = normalize_to_sum(a0, float(np.sqrt(2.0 * max(1, a0.size))))
        return a0, fast_objective(a0)

    n = int(a0.size)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    a = normalize_to_sum(a0, S)

    c0 = convolve_full(a)
    best_obj = float(c0.max())
    if not np.isfinite(best_obj):
        best_obj = 1e300
    best_a = a.copy()

    now = time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= now:
        return best_a, best_obj

    eta = 0.30
    beta = 5.0
    max_beta = 30000.0
    temp = 1e-4
    prev = best_obj
    progress = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = now

    # Cache to avoid recomputing the same q/H when only eta changes.
    a_version = 0
    version_cache = -1
    q_cache = None
    qmax_cache = float("inf")
    h_cache = None
    beta_cache = -1.0
    mean_cache = 0.0

    if label:
        print("optimize start %s n=%d obj=%.7f" % (label, n, best_obj))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1

        if it % 256 < 20:
            beta_use = 1.0
        else:
            beta_use = beta
            if progress > 80:
                beta_use = min(max_beta, beta_use * (1.0 + 0.01 * min(100, progress - 80)))

        if a_version == version_cache and q_cache is not None:
            q = q_cache
            cmax = qmax_cache
        else:
            q = convolve_full(a)
            cmax = float(q.max())
            if not np.isfinite(cmax):
                a = normalize_to_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n), S)
                a_version += 1
                q_cache = None
                version_cache = -1
                h_cache = None
                beta_cache = -1.0
                continue
            q_cache = q
            qmax_cache = cmax
            version_cache = a_version

        if cmax < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = cmax
            best_a = a.copy()

        if cmax < prev - 1e-12:
            progress = 0
        else:
            progress += 1
        prev = cmax

        if beta_use != beta_cache or h_cache is None:
            w = softmax_weights(q, beta_use)
            h = hankel_product(a, w)
            mean_h = float(np.mean(h)) if h.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(mean_h) or mean_h < 1e-300:
                h_cache = None
                beta_cache = -1.0
                mean_cache = 0.0
            else:
                h_cache = h
                beta_cache = beta_use
                mean_cache = mean_h

        if h_cache is None:
            a = normalize_to_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n), S)
            a_version += 1
            continue

        h = h_cache
        mean_h = mean_cache

        candidates = []
        d = (h - mean_h) / mean_h
        d = np.clip(d, -5.0, 5.0)

        m1 = max(0.01, eta * 0.3)
        m2 = float(eta)
        m3 = min(4.0, eta * 4.0)
        for em in (m1, m2, m3):
            arg = -em * d
            arg = np.clip(arg, -18.0, 18.0)
            cand = a * np.exp(arg)
            cand = normalize_to_sum(cand, S)
            candidates.append(cand)

        if it % 32 == 0:
            g = h - mean_h
            normg = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if normg > 1e-20:
                scale = S / max(normg, S / float(n))
                for lm in (0.05, 0.25):
                    cand = a - (lm * scale) * g
                    cand = project_simplex(cand, S)
                    cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                    cand += 1e-12 * S / float(n)
                    cand = normalize_to_sum(cand, S)
                    candidates.append(cand)

        old_cur = cmax
        best_cand = None
        best_cand_obj = old_cur

        for cand in candidates:
            c2 = convolve_full(cand)
            o2 = float(c2.max())
            if not np.isfinite(o2):
                continue
            if o2 < best_cand_obj - 1e-13:
                best_cand_obj = o2
                best_cand = cand
            if o2 < best_obj - 1e-13:
                best_obj = o2
                best_a = cand.copy()

        if best_cand is not None and (best_cand_obj < old_cur - 1e-13 or best_cand_obj < old_cur + temp):
            a = best_cand
            a_version += 1
            if best_cand_obj < old_cur - 1e-13:
                progress = 0
                eta = min(2.0, eta * 1.05)
                beta = min(max_beta, beta * 1.02)
                temp = min(5e-4, temp * 1.1)
            else:
                temp = max(1e-7, temp * 0.5)
            prev = best_cand_obj

        else:
            if eta > 0.05:
                eta *= 0.7
            else:
                eta = min(2.0, eta * 1.5)
            if progress > 50:
                beta = min(max_beta, beta * 1.1)

        if progress > 350:
            best_pert = None
            best_pert_obj = cmax
            for _ in range(5):
                r = float(rng.random())
                if r < 0.2:
                    cand = 0.7 * a + 0.3 * best_a
                elif r < 0.5:
                    scale = 0.02 * S / max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(n)))
                    cand = a + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
                elif r < 0.8:
                    rand = random_positive_sequence(n, rng, S)
                    cand = 0.8 * a + 0.2 * rand
                else:
                    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, n)
                    cand = a * np.exp(np.clip(noise, -6.0, 6.0))
                    cand += 1e-10 * S / float(n)

                cand = normalize_to_sum(cand, S)
                c2 = convolve_full(cand)
                o2 = float(c2.max())
                if np.isfinite(o2) and o2 < best_pert_obj:
                    best_pert_obj = o2
                    best_pert = cand

            if best_pert is not None:
                a = best_pert
                a_version += 1
                prev = best_pert_obj
                if best_pert_obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
                    best_obj = best_pert_obj
                    best_a = a.copy()

            beta = max(1.0, beta * 0.4)
            eta = 0.3
            temp = 3e-4
            progress = 100

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            print("opt %s it=%d best=%.7f cur=%.7f prog=%d beta=%.1f eta=%.3f temp=%.2e"
                  % (label, it, best_obj, cmax, progress, beta, eta, temp))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    c = convolve_full(best_a)
    fin = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(fin) or fin > best_obj:
        fin = best_obj
    return best_a, fin


def propose_candidate(seed=2024, budget_s=2400):
    """Return the best nonnegative sequence found within budget_s seconds."""
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start = time.monotonic()
    global_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - 60.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_obj = float("inf")
    best_a = None

    given = None
    try:
        g = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if g.size >= 64:
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(g.sum()) > 1e-6:
                if g.size > 16384:
                    g = resample_sequence(g, 16384)
                given = g
    except Exception:
        given = None

    rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 30:
        a = power_sequence(4096, 0.5, False, np.sqrt(2.0 * 4096))
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    # Stage 1: optimize the existing construction if available, otherwise a
    # strong built-in asymmetric power sequence.
    if given is not None:
        n1 = int(given.size)
        init1 = given.copy()
        if rem < 150:
            dur1 = rem * 0.8
        else:
            dur1 = max(60.0, min(rem * 0.35, rem - 60.0))
    else:
        n1 = 8192 if rem > 1000 else 4096
        init1 = power_sequence(n1, 0.5, False, np.sqrt(2.0 * n1))
        if rem < 150:
            dur1 = rem * 0.8
        else:
            dur1 = max(90.0, min(rem * 0.45, rem - 60.0))

    dur1 = max(10.0, min(dur1, rem - 5.0))
    a1, s1 = optimize_sequence(init1, time.monotonic() + dur1, rng, "stage1")
    best_obj, best_a = update_global(a1, best_obj, best_a)
    if best_a is not None:
        print("after stage1 n=%d obj=%.7f" % (len(best_a), best_obj))

    # Stage 2: upscale to a larger length and continue polishing.
    rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and rem > 180 and best_a.size < 16384:
        n2 = min(16384, max(1024, int(np.ceil(best_a.size * 1.5))))
        if n2 <= best_a.size:
            n2 = min(16384, int(best_a.size) + 4096)
        init2 = resample_sequence(best_a, n2)
        if rem < 300:
            dur2 = rem * 0.6
        else:
            dur2 = max(90.0, min(rem * 0.55, rem - 120.0))
        dur2 = max(10.0, min(dur2, rem - 5.0))
        a2, s2 = optimize_sequence(init2, time.monotonic() + dur2, rng, "stage2")
        best_obj, best_a = update_global(a2, best_obj, best_a)
        if best_a is not None:
            print("after stage2 n=%d obj=%.7f" % (len(best_a), best_obj))

    # Stage 3: one more increase in length if we are not already at the cap.
    rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and rem > 150 and best_a.size < 16384:
        n3 = min(16384, max(int(best_a.size) * 2, int(best_a.size) + 4096))
        init3 = resample_sequence(best_a, n3)
        if rem < 300:
            dur3 = rem * 0.5
        else:
            dur3 = max(60.0, min(rem * 0.6, rem - 60.0))
        dur3 = max(10.0, min(dur3, rem - 5.0))
        a3, s3 = optimize_sequence(init3, time.monotonic() + dur3, rng, "stage3")
        best_obj, best_a = update_global(a3, best_obj, best_a)
        if best_a is not None:
            print("after stage3 n=%d obj=%.7f" % (len(best_a), best_obj))

    # Final polishing stage.
    rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and rem > 30:
        final_deadline = global_deadline - max(5.0, rem * 0.05)
        af, sf = optimize_sequence(best_a, final_deadline, rng, "final")
        best_obj, best_a = update_global(af, best_obj, best_a)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = power_sequence(4096, 0.5, False, np.sqrt(2.0 * 4096))

    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_a.size == 0 or float(best_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        best_a = power_sequence(4096, 0.5, False, np.sqrt(2.0 * 4096))

    print("final length=%d internal=%.7f elapsed=%.1f"
          % (len(best_a), fast_objective(best_a), time.monotonic() - start))

    # Optional true-evaluator sanity check for moderate lengths.
    if best_a.size <= 8192 and time.monotonic() < global_deadline - 30:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
            print("evaluate_sequence check:", ev)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
